<header><h1><a href="/article/46439/random-set-of-the-day-puck-passer">Random set of the day: Puck Passer</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/Huwbot" rel="author">Huwbot</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-23 02:00">23 Oct 2019 02:00</time></small></header><a class="highslide plain" href="https://images.brickset.com/sets//images/3540-1.jpg" onclick="return hs.expand(this)"><img alt="Puck Passer" class="mainimg" src="https://images.brickset.com/sets/small/3540-1.jpg"/></a><div class="highslide-caption"><h1>Puck Passer</h1><div class="tags floatleft"><a href="/sets/3540-1/Puck-Passer">3540-1</a> <a href="/sets/theme-Sports">Sports</a> <a class="subtheme" href="/sets/theme-Sports/subtheme-Hockey">Hockey</a> <a class="year" href="/sets/theme-Sports/year-2003">2003</a> </div><div class="floatright">©2003 LEGO Group</div></div><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Random-set">Random set</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/46439/random-set-of-the-day-puck-passer#comments">5 comments</a></div><div class="meta">131 Views</div></footer>
<p>Today's random set is <a href="/sets/3540-1">3540</a> Puck Passer, released in 2003. It's one of <a href="/sets/theme-Sports/year-2003">52 Sports sets</a> produced that year. It contains <a href="/inventories/3540-1">44 pieces</a>, and its retail price was US$8/£4.99. </p><p>It's owned by 475 Brickset members. If you want to add it to your collection you might find it for sale at <a href="https://www.bricklink.com/v2/catalog/catalogitem.page?S=3540-1#T=S" rel="nofollow" title="Link to external website, opens in a new tab.">BrickLink</a> or <a href="/buy/ebay/?query=3540-1">eBay</a>.</p><p><a href="https://brickset.com/article/46439/random-set-of-the-day-puck-passer">Continue reading »</a></p>

<article class="post">
<header><h1><a href="/article/46423/controlling-the-liebherr-r-9800">Controlling the Liebherr R 9800</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/Huw" rel="author">Huw</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-22 17:00">22 Oct 2019 17:00</time></small></header><a class="highslide" href="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48838022181_b5960e8835_b.jpg" onclick="return hs.expand(this)"><img class="mainimg" src="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48838022181_b5960e8835_n.jpg"/></a><span class="highslide-caption"><a href="https://www.flickr.com/photos/brickset/48838022181/in/dateposted/" rel="nofollow" title="Link to external website, opens in a new tab.">View image at flickr</a></span><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Set-review">Set review</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/46423/controlling-the-liebherr-r-9800#comments">19 comments</a></div><div class="meta">2332 Views</div></footer>
<p>I started reviewing <a href="https://brickset.com/sets/42100-1">42100</a> Liebherr R 9800 a couple of weeks ago and in <a href="https://brickset.com/article/46030/review-42100-liebherr-r9800">part one</a> I covered construction and the completed model.</p>
<p>However, that's only half the story because the digger is entirely dependent on a smartphone app to function: it's just an expensive ornament without it.</p>
<p>Thankfully, the Control+ app is very good indeed. It enables you to operate and control the model like never before.</p>
<p><a href="https://brickset.com/article/46423/controlling-the-liebherr-r-9800">Continue reading »</a></p>

<header><h1><a href="/article/46422/brick-fanatics-magazine-issue-11-out-now">Brick Fanatics magazine issue 11 out now</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/Huw" rel="author">Huw</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-22 11:00">22 Oct 2019 11:00</time></small></header><a href="http://www.brickfanatics.com/magazine?wpam_id=3" rel="nofollow" title="Link to external website, opens in a new tab."><img alt="" class="mainimg" src="https://images.brickset.com/news/thumbs/large/tn_Brick%20Fanatics%20Magazine%20Issue%2011%20cover_20191022_jpg.jpg"/></a><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Magazines">Magazines</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/46422/brick-fanatics-magazine-issue-11-out-now#comments">2 comments</a></div><div class="meta">525 Views</div></footer>
<p>Here's the press release:</p>
<p>Brick Fanatics Magazine Issue 11 includes a detailed LEGO designer walkthrough of 75252 Imperial Star Destroyer, in a latest collaboration with Brickset</p>
<p>The third largest ever LEGO Star Wars set adorns the latest LEGO magazine’s front cover as the subject of a very special feature inside.</p>
<p>Alongside eye-catching photography, we highlight some of the most interesting facts and build techniques that this Ultimate Collector Series set has to offer. At the same time, designer Henrik Andersen shares some fascinating insight into the detail and design that went into creating 75252, exclusively in conversation with <em>Brickset</em>, presented across the pages of <em>Brick Fanatics Magazine</em>.</p>

<header><h1><a href="/article/46404/lego-to-sell-wooden-toys-again">LEGO to sell wooden toys again</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/Huw" rel="author">Huw</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-21 21:00">21 Oct 2019 21:00</time></small></header><img alt="" class="mainimg" src="https://images.brickset.com/news/thumbs/large/tn_LEGO_VIP_Originals_Banner_03_0c6cb7_jpg.jpg"/><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Miscellaneous">Miscellaneous</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/46404/lego-to-sell-wooden-toys-again#comments">56 comments</a></div><div class="meta">9536 Views</div></footer>
<p>LEGO is opening a pop-up shop in Covent Garden, London at the beginning of November at which attendees will have the opportunity to "be the first to own the brand new wooden LEGO collectable."</p>
<p>VIPs were invited to an event on the 1st and 2nd of November by email earlier today but the limited number of places available have now all gone.</p>
<p>As far as I can tell 2019 and 2020 are not significant anniversaries of the first LEGO wooden toy, or the iconic wooden duck, so I don't know what it is or why it's being produced now.</p>
<p>Whatever it is, it's either big or made with premium materials, because it'll set you back a cool £109.99...</p>
<p>So, speculate away in the comments.</p>
<div class="links">

<article class="post">
<header><h1><a href="/article/46321/review-40352-scarecrow">Review: 40352 Scarecrow</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/Huw" rel="author">Huw</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-21 16:00">21 Oct 2019 16:00</time></small></header><a class="highslide" href="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48917710671_865575287e_b.jpg" onclick="return hs.expand(this)"><img class="mainimg" src="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48917710671_865575287e_n.jpg"/></a><span class="highslide-caption"><a href="https://www.flickr.com/photos/brickset/48917710671/in/dateposted/" rel="nofollow" title="Link to external website, opens in a new tab.">View image at flickr</a></span><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Set-review">Set review</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/46321/review-40352-scarecrow#comments">17 comments</a></div><div class="meta">2118 Views</div></footer>
<p>This year's <a href="https://brickset.com/sets/theme-BrickHeadz/subtheme-Seasonal/year-2019">seasonal BrickHeadz</a> have on the whole been excellent and now that we are not being bombarded with dozens of the blocky characters every few weeks it is very welcome when a new one becomes available.</p>
<p>The Christmas one has just hit the brand store shelves but before we get ahead of ourselves, let's take a look at <a href="https://brickset.com/sets/40352-1">40352</a> Scarecrow which was released a few weeks ago, in good time for Thanksgiving in the USA and Canada.</p>
<p><a href="https://brickset.com/article/46321/review-40352-scarecrow">Continue reading »</a></p>

<article class="post">
<header><h1><a href="/article/45879/review-75255-yoda">Review: 75255 Yoda</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/CapnRex101" rel="author">CapnRex101</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-20 16:30">20 Oct 2019 16:30</time></small></header><a class="highslide" href="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48893070521_486104fee1_b.jpg" onclick="return hs.expand(this)"><img class="mainimg" src="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48893070521_486104fee1_n.jpg"/></a><span class="highslide-caption"><a href="https://www.flickr.com/photos/brickset/48893070521/in/photostream/" rel="nofollow" title="Link to external website, opens in a new tab.">View image at flickr</a></span><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Set-review">Set review</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/45879/review-75255-yoda#comments">40 comments</a></div><div class="meta">8961 Views</div></footer>
<p>Some marvellous large scale models have been produced recently and <a href="https://brickset.com/sets/75255-1/%7B-%7D">75255</a> Yoda seems quite impressive. This interesting rendition of the Jedi Master includes magnificent detail and appears accurate when compared with its famed source material. However, certain aspects of its design have elicited criticism.</p>
<p>The eyes have proven particularly controversial based upon their unusual aesthetic in some official images. However, they convey wonderful expression when properly positioned and should represent an improvement over <a href="https://brickset.com/sets/7194-1/Yoda">7194</a> Yoda from 2002. Hopefully this model will match the high standard of other large-scale characters.</p>
<p><a href="https://brickset.com/article/45879/review-75255-yoda">Continue reading »</a></p>

<header><h1><a href="/article/46368/random-set-of-the-day-analysis">Random set of the day analysis</a></h1><small>Posted by <a href="/profile/Huwbot" rel="author">Huwbot</a>, <time datetime="2019-10-20 15:00">20 Oct 2019 15:00</time></small></header><a class="highslide" href="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48412806617_0fb74f999b_b.jpg" onclick="return hs.expand(this)"><img class="mainimg" src="https://live.staticflickr.com/65535/48412806617_0fb74f999b_n.jpg"/></a><span class="highslide-caption"><a href="https://www.flickr.com/photos/kumipallomaa/48412806617/in/pool-14640859@N23/" rel="nofollow" title="Link to external website, opens in a new tab.">View image at flickr</a></span><footer><div class="tags"><a href="/news/category-Brickset">Brickset</a></div> <div class="comments"><a href="/article/46368/random-set-of-the-day-analysis#comments">59 comments</a></div><div class="meta">6947 Views</div></footer>
<p>Good afternoon! Huwbot here! I see that there's some absurd speculation on the random set of the day article today alleging that I pick Clikits more often than sets from other themes.</p>
<p>So, I've gathered the data and crunched the numbers to see if this is the case. As it turns out, it seems that I do pick them more often than I should, in accordance with my preference...</p>
